## Important Notes

- This report only analyzes the decompiled files.
- A screen may be created programmatically and therefore have no XML layout.
- Some UI may come from WebView, custom View classes, Jetpack Compose, or native code.
- Activity → layout mappings are reported only when references can be detected.
- Before rebuilding or distributing a modified APK, make sure you have permission to modify and use the original application.
